cua-spaces-v0.21.0Cua Spaces v0.21.0 Stable2026-11-06T13:52:16Z

Cua Spaces 0.21.0

Summary

This release adds 10 features and includes 2 fixes.

Features

  • ask first, CI runners, TCP shares, secure networks and the phone app, behind experiments. (#6355)
  • warm pools, a prepared Linux Space and agent harnesses ready in about one second (behind the warm-pools experiment). (#6472)
  • pick up where you left off in the first run (CUA-3319). (#6463)
  • the new prompt box with a model and an optional repo. (#6490)
  • Cua Driver built into Cua Spaces (CUA-2771, CUA-2775). (#6505)
  • Spaces as a tree and a default Space, behind Labs, with the SDK bindings and CLI (CUA-3413). (#6508)
  • the warming home stays out of the top bar and off the phone until a person writes into it. (#6513)
  • Stable / Nightly update channel; betas move to Nightly (CUA-3503). (#6548)
  • permissions step in the new first run, one permission flow with the Cua Driver page (CUA-3550). (#6579)
  • Network picker with an "Also allow" list (localhost and local network are siblings). (#6610)

Fixes

  • the macOS release build installs Go for the network gateway. (#6422)
  • Cua Driver runs after the new first run, whatever the Mac shares (CUA-3485). (#6534)

Contributors

This release contains maintainer changes only.

lume-v0.7.0lume: v0.7.0 Stable2026-11-06T13:17:15Z

Lume 0.7.0

Summary

This release adds 4 features and includes 10 fixes.

Features

  • add lume cp for host↔guest file transfer. (#4896)
  • compat gateways for E2B and Daytona, Hetzner as your own cloud, the elastic pool, cua share and the public relay. (#5778)
  • install source builds as lume-local, beside the released lume. (#5804)
  • ask first, CI runners, TCP shares, secure networks and the phone app, behind experiments. (#6355)

Fixes

  • stop a VM in lume serve without ending the server. (#4662)
  • preserve legacy multipart disk bytes during assembly. (#4714) Thanks @cooleryu.
  • preserve background service during updates. (#4337) Thanks @Iflaqbhat.
  • shell-escape remote command arguments in lume ssh (#3879). (#3911) Thanks @shobhitagnihotri69; reported by @jmgilman.
  • refuse a macOS guest start at the host's macOS guest limit. (#3958) Thanks @lorenzozanee; reported by @jmgilman.
  • report a VM stopped once its guest stops on its own. (#4853) reported by @coygeek.
  • pull parameterized OCI tar disk parts. (#5192) Thanks @cooleryu; reported by @zpv.
  • print VM details as labeled fields in terminals. (#5358) Thanks @0xABAN.
  • verify VM owner before stopping. (#5611)
  • reach macOS VMs over vsock, not Local Network or VPN routes (CUA-3139). (#6380)

Contributors

Thanks to @0xABAN, @cooleryu, @coygeek, @Iflaqbhat, @jmgilman, @lorenzozanee, @shobhitagnihotri69, @zpv for contributing to this release.

Install

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/lume/install.sh)"

Lume releases include a notarized macOS command-line archive and installer package, plus stable alias filenames for the install script.

cua-driver-rs-v0.39.0cua-driver-rs: v0.39.0 Prerelease2026-11-06T12:33:37Z

Cua Driver 0.39.0

Summary

This release adds 9 features and includes 67 fixes.

Features

  • compat gateways for E2B and Daytona, Hetzner as your own cloud, the elastic pool, cua share and the public relay. (#5778)
  • let macOS actions skip the post-action window-change wait. (#6033) Thanks @gakonst.
  • add JPEG screenshots to get_window_state and encode from raw pixels. (#6042) Thanks @gakonst.
  • plain, upright agent cursor by default; effects and marks opt-in. (#6142)
  • add a niri compositor adapter for Linux Wayland. (#6209) Thanks @ayagmar, @injaneity.
  • cursor floats while moving, soft halo, lean/heading rotation modes. (#6325)
  • fast path with act-time target guards, behind a default-off flag (CUA-3102). (#6332)
  • opt-in refusal of ungranted macOS Automation scripting. (#5777) reported by @nick-inkeep.
  • capture embedded startup diagnostics. (#3281)

Fixes

  • keys for a popup an MPX click opened go through the master keyboard of the session that clicked. (#5652) Thanks @CMGS.
  • the macOS WindowServer health check treats any app-bundle host as bundled. (#5680)
  • guard Space-free background window enumeration. (#5712) Thanks @CMGS.
  • stabilize the known flaky release-gate tests. (#5538)
  • build client-kind SDK constructors without rebuilding the driver. (#5896)
  • give the DOM-click baseline readback a longer budget. (#5918)
  • remove zero-caller macOS drag and AXValue helpers. (#5962)
  • remove zero-caller input, portal screencast, sway and overlay functions. (#5958)
  • inline identical refusal forwarders. (#5963)
  • delete zero-caller API in cua-driver-core and cua-driver. (#5815)
  • refuse Windows Chromium clicks from a minimized-window snapshot. (#5686) Thanks @philipljh.
  • treat Windows elements read while minimized as stale on resolver-routed clicks. (#6049)
  • remove dead macOS active-Space helper and single-use snapshot wrapper. (#6094)
  • drop the unused test-only capture registry action API. (#6114)
  • share overlay query reads across Linux and Windows. (#6117)
  • locate Finder by name on macOS. (#5390) Thanks @abonneth.
  • map Wayland punctuation shortcut keys. (#4811) Thanks @louistiti.
  • restore Windows cursor after foreground window clicks. (#5772) Thanks @BhaveshY.
  • honor modifiers during Windows drags. (#5667) Thanks @stanley50z.
  • await synthetic smooth-scroll readback. (#4602) Thanks @Iflaqbhat.
  • share the keyring history key provider in cua-driver-core. (#5895) Thanks @injaneity.
  • resolve Windows press_key characters on the target's layout. (#6201) Thanks @injaneity; reported by @MankhongGarden.
  • deliver libei pointer input to secondary monitors. (#5853) Thanks @injaneity; reported by @Faraclas.
  • report denied Linux portal screenshots instead of falling back to X11. (#6124)
  • share the DevTools /json/version probe across browser adapters. (#6098)
  • report the current frontmost app on macOS without the overlay. (#6182) Thanks @injaneity; reported by @lucasfdale.
  • name current snapshots on a token-only refusal, and refuse a bare row a re-read orphaned. (#6259)
  • stop the Linux focus restore when the saved window is gone. (#5596) Thanks @CMGS, @injaneity.
  • count only the returned elements in an elements diff read. (#5649) Thanks @skap3214.
  • never borrow keycode 8 for a Linux text character. (#5595) Thanks @CMGS.
  • a file write on the Cua Volume mount no longer fails after the file is in place. (#6322) reported by @stanley50z, @ymqy.
  • draw the GNOME helper cursor's tip on the click point. (#6344)
  • stop the X11 agent cursor splitting in two while it moves. (#6343)
  • quarantine the linux-kvm concurrent flake, fix the TCC seed lock and the Windows portmap pick. (#6365)
  • restore the X11 active window by bouncing the core focus when only the WM's record moved. (#5651) Thanks @CMGS.
  • record KDE Wayland desktops through the portal ScreenCast. (#6361) Thanks @factory-ain3sh.
  • scope bounded launch_app to resources.apps launch grants. (#5690) Thanks @Katechnology; reported by @ShadyElsharnoby.
  • paint the macOS cursor overlay as a region, not a full-screen frame. (#5650) Thanks @CMGS.
  • expose legacy macOS page fallback. (#5590)
  • collapse daemon-proxy spawn and test the macOS proxy path. (#5812)
  • stop unit tests mutating the process environment. (#6417)
  • keep browser refusal codes through protected scope attestation. (#5573) Thanks @abonneth.
  • list bound tabs in the order the browser reports them. (#5527) Thanks @abonneth.
  • tell picom's damage lag from a torn frame in the X11 overlay test. (#6431)
  • report a sent but unanswered AT-SPI option pick as may-have-landed. (#6445)
  • time the batch pause test on a paused clock. (#6453)
  • treat a niri focus read-back the deadline cut off as unconfirmed. (#6436)
  • stamp the window-local point on macOS 27 background clicks. (#5492) Thanks @cristicretu.
  • stop the Node runtime leaking a libuv handle per future poll. (#5691) Thanks @jhugman, @smraikai.
  • keep browser attestation off the renderer while a dialog is open. (#5524) Thanks @abonneth.
  • index disabled macOS controls and report enabled: false. (#5635) Thanks @kurtguenther; reported by @will-bogusz.
  • remove stale screenshot tool references. (#2317)
  • return from a dom_event click when its handler opens a dialog. (#5621) Thanks @sahiljagtap08.
  • clear clippy -D warnings on main. (#6461)
  • bind every sdk test socket under a short /tmp dir. (#6466)
  • force embedded mode in the SDK in-process loader tests. (#6485)
  • normalize list_windows z-index parity. (#2172)
  • detect Hyprland input internals instead of pinning 0.56.2. (#6495) Thanks @chrisohearn.
  • refuse with 'did not verify' when cosign cannot start in install.ps1. (#6483) Thanks @sly321-pixel.
  • add SPDX headers to cua-driver sources (CUA-3388). (#6516)
  • treat niri workspace read-backs the cleanup deadline cut off as unconfirmed. (#6520)
  • keep a full budget for each dom_event click readback. (#6519)
  • initialize recording hooks for embedded platform registries. (#5785) reported by @nick-inkeep.
  • keep cua-driver-core clippy-clean without default features. (#6543)
  • raise own-process windows on the AppKit main queue (CUA-3309). (#6559)
  • return from a trusted browser click when its handler opens a dialog. (#6562)
  • Windows type_text posts UTF-16 units in order and repairs dropped tails (CUA-3122). (#6341)

Performance

  • build the tool contract manifest once per process. (#5977) Thanks @gakonst.

Contributors

Thanks to @abonneth, @ayagmar, @BhaveshY, @chrisohearn, @CMGS, @cristicretu, @factory-ain3sh, @Faraclas, @gakonst, @Iflaqbhat, @jhugman, @Katechnology, @kurtguenther, @louistiti, @lucasfdale, @MankhongGarden, @nick-inkeep, @philipljh, @sahiljagtap08, @ShadyElsharnoby, @skap3214, @sly321-pixel, @smraikai, @stanley50z, @will-bogusz, @ymqy for contributing to this release.

Install

macOS and Linux:

/bin/bash -c "$(curl -fsSL https://cua.ai/driver/install.sh)"

Windows:

irm https://cua.ai/driver/install.ps1 | iex

Cua Driver ships universal macOS binaries, x86_64 and arm64 Windows builds, and Linux preview builds. Release assets also include tag-pinned installer and uninstaller scripts.

Why GitHub says “Pre-release”

GitHub's label is used only to keep this monorepo's repository-wide “Latest” pointer from switching between independently released products. A plain Cua Driver SemVer is a stable release; npm and PyPI publish it on their normal stable channels, and the installers resolve the versioned cua-driver-rs-v* releases directly.

  • Fix: return from a dom_event click when its handler opens a dialog. (#5621) Thanks @sahiljagtap08.
  • Fix: clear clippy -D warnings on main. (#6461)
  • Fix: bind every sdk test socket under a short /tmp dir. (#6466)
  • Docs: run the macOS Lume lane from the verify skill. (#6476)
  • Fix: force embedded mode in the SDK in-process loader tests. (#6485)
  • Fix: normalize list_windows z-index parity. (#2172)
  • Fix: detect Hyprland input internals instead of pinning 0.56.2. (#6495) Thanks @chrisohearn.
  • Fix: refuse with 'did not verify' when cosign cannot start in install.ps1. (#6483) Thanks @sly321-pixel.

<!-- macos-install-note -->

macOS install note

The app isn't signed with an Apple Developer ID yet, so macOS may call a downloaded build "damaged" — it's Gatekeeper quarantine, not a bad file. After moving the app to Applications, clear the flag once, then open normally:

xattr -dr com.apple.quarantine "/Applications/OpenHands Agent Canvas.app"

Signing + notarization is tracked in OpenHands/OpenHands#16774.

ctx7@0.10.0 Stable2026-11-06T12:16:16Z

Minor Changes

  • 9783083: Add Pi to ctx7 setup and ctx7 remove. ctx7 setup --pi writes the Context7 MCP server to ~/.pi/agent/mcp.json (or .pi/mcp.json with --project) with direct tool exposure. It also adds the Context7 rule to Pi's context file and installs the skill in ~/.agents/skills. Pi reads one context file per folder, so setup adds the rule to an existing AGENTS.override.md or CLAUDE.md instead of creating an AGENTS.md that would hide it. Setup honors PI_CODING_AGENT_DIR, and with --oauth it prints the pi mcp login context7 step.

Patch Changes

  • 505b812: Keep your comments when ctx7 setup or ctx7 remove touches an MCP config. .vscode/mcp.json and .cursor/mcp.json are JSONC, and the CLI accepted them for reading but wrote them back through JSON.stringify, so every comment and any layout you kept in the file was silently dropped. The Context7 entry is now added or removed with an in-place edit that leaves the rest of the file alone, comments included. A file the edit cannot handle safely, such as one whose trailing comma would be stranded, is still rewritten whole as before.
  • db66f7a: Fix ctx7 setup and ctx7 remove leaving the Context7 section in an AGENTS.md or GEMINI.md that has CRLF line endings.
  • ff0422f: Add a T3 Code setup choice and --t3 setup/remove flag that configure its Claude Code and Codex providers.

View release →

[3.0.0-alpha.6]

Bug Fixes

  • 78f49ba39 (#16077 by @amrbashir) tauri now holds the per-window/per-webview event listeners itself and feeds them from the run event loop, which fixes Window::on_window_event and Webview::on_webview_event missing events fired before the registration reached the event loop.
  • 78f49ba39 (#16077 by @amrbashir) Fixed Webview::close on a handle to an already closed webview tearing down the state and event listeners of a newer webview reusing its label.
  • 78f49ba39 (#16077 by @amrbashir) Fixed a window recreated under the same label while the previous one is being destroyed (from a WindowEvent::Destroyed handler or a plugin's on_event) losing the event listeners registered on it in the meantime.

Dependencies

  • Upgraded to tauri-runtime@3.0.0-alpha.5

[3.0.0-alpha.5]

Breaking Changes

  • 78f49ba39 (#16077 by @amrbashir) Removed WindowDispatch::on_window_event and WebviewDispatch::on_webview_event, along with the WindowEventId and WebviewEventId types. Runtimes already report every window and webview event through RunEvent::WindowEvent / RunEvent::WebviewEvent, so the per-window listener registries each runtime kept were a duplicate of that stream.

    Migration: custom runtimes should drop their on_window_event/on_webview_event implementations and their listener storage; emitting RunEvent::WindowEvent and RunEvent::WebviewEvent is now the only thing required to deliver events.

thedotmack/claude-mem ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v15.10.1 Stable2026-11-06T11:52:02Z

A bug-fix release. No new settings and no breaking changes.

Reliability

  • Context reads keep working when the database's -wal and wal-index files are missing. (#5432 by @0xABCD01)
  • Queued batches are kept, not dropped, when Claude replies with a 429 rate limit. (#5430 by @markjoin123)
  • A stalled session's buffered work is discarded once its resume attempts are used up, so it no longer blocks the queue. (#5414 by @0xABCD01)
  • SSE frames queued for a slow consumer are now bounded. (#5395 by @liuyinchu)
  • The observer response deadline has its own constant. (#5406 by @lunetics)
  • The observer fills missing proxy settings from your Claude user settings. (#5401 by @markjoin123)
  • /api/processing-status reports sessions the stall pacer abandoned and the observer's last successful store time. (#5409, #5408 by @xunguangzlj-cloud)
  • Settings: SessionStart toggles persist, and zero context counts are accepted via POST /api/settings. (#5393 by @0xABCD01)

Security

  • All TeamBrain routes reject requests from foreign origins. (#5411 by @DeryFerd)
  • Perplexity API keys are redacted. (#5407 by @mertkayacs)

Viewer and My Work

  • My Work sharing stays on for the cloud viewer. (#5433 by @thedotmack)
  • Unparseable work-view frames are dropped instead of throwing. (#5412 by @DeryFerd)
  • Hosted usage reaches PostHog again (randomUUID is called on crypto). (#5421 by @thedotmack)
  • Windows file paths are shortened on observation cards. (#5419 by @Plastic-time)

Search and smart-read

  • Dumb search keeps fetchable memories ahead of prompts. (#5397 by @liuyinchu)
  • Search recalls supplementary Han and extended Katakana characters. (#5396 by @liuyinchu)
  • Outlines include C and C++ unions, and Zig struct, enum, union and opaque declarations. (#5403, #5402 by @L4XB)

Install, CLI and platforms

  • The installer writes MCP JSON configs atomically. (#5413 by @DeryFerd)
  • Agent-driven installs are guided into the user's browser setup, and the docs list the terminal prompts an agent must relay. (#5379 by @thedotmack)
  • Cursor: reads and searches are recorded through Cursor's postToolUse hook. (#5399 by @Lumea-art)
  • --help after a verb shows help instead of running the command. (#5394 by @Bortlesboat)
  • Windows: the data directory removal command works, and integration tests stay out of the fast lane. (#5420, #5418 by @Plastic-time)

Full changelog: https://github.com/thedotmack/claude-mem/compare/v15.10.0...v15.10.1

View release →

v15.10.0 Stable2026-11-05T19:57:39Z

What's new

Onboarding finds your existing work. Setup now leads with discovering your existing work, rules and focus from your transcripts. The main button is "Discover My Work, Rules & Focus", and "Skip this step" is a smaller action under it. The "Share anonymous usage statistics to help improve your Claude-mem experience" box starts checked; unchecking it is kept, and nothing is sent until you press Discover. When hosted setup is busy, claude-mem checks capacity before uploading, remembers the cooldown across restarts and lets you finish setup. (#5270, #5308, #5384 by @thedotmack)

Turn memory capture off. New CLAUDE_MEM_OBSERVER_ENABLED setting and a Memory capture toggle. Set it to "false" to stop new observation and summary model calls while keeping existing memories, search and session-start context. (#5353 by @tarunbad)

Pick the observer per tool. New CLAUDE_MEM_PROVIDER_BY_SOURCE setting picks the observer provider per session source, for example Codex sessions on Codex and Claude Code sessions on Claude. (#5333 by @ankit4479)

Long-lived Claude login. ~/.claude-mem/.env can now supply CLAUDE_CODE_OAUTH_TOKEN, so capture doesn't stop when the stored Claude Code login expires. Sessions paused for auth resume once a credential is shown to work. (#5372 by @MeggDistribution, #5383 by @xunguangzlj-cloud)

Combine modes. Mode overrides now chain, so a language mode can combine with chill, like code--chill--ko. (#5349 by @BaeJinSeop)

Doctor explains a missing observer. npx claude-mem doctor now reports an unavailable observer provider and the worker's dependencies, instead of saying all is well. (#5366 by @parveen0029)

Viewer sync and My Work. Viewer sync recovery is now on main: faster bounded views, truthful sync status, shared My Work state between devices, and recovery of remote changes. On the cloud viewer, My Work links to Account & billing. (#5410, #5416 by @thedotmack)

Reliability

  • The worker waits for a free slot instead of failing when the pool of 10 is full, and releases an idle slot when other sessions are waiting. (#5386 by @balabommablock-cpu, #5388 by @0xABCD01)
  • Hung context-reader requests now time out, so a stuck read no longer stops observations. (#5387 by @markjoin123)
  • Failure tracking restarts when the kind of failure changes. (#5380 by @xunguangzlj-cloud)
  • Codex exits are classified only from their recent stderr. (#5310 by @2023Anita)
  • The observer no longer inherits a parent gateway's model names, and keeps your Claude beta opt-out. A model refusal is reported as a model outage. (#5316 by @sayedrisat, #5317 by @parveen0029, #5348 by @tarunbad)
  • SSE streams keep their framing across chunk boundaries, and the viewer ignores frames it can't parse. (#5367 by @Afloat16, #5364 by @DeryFerd)
  • Capped error-body readers release their locks. (#5340 by @Afloat16)
  • Bug reports fall back to the template when the SDK doesn't finish a report. (#5302 by @liuyinchu)

Install and platforms

  • Windows: the worker daemon starts without PowerShell. (#5351 by @REI-330)
  • Copilot CLI: the MCP server is registered in ~/.copilot/mcp-config.json, where Copilot CLI reads it. (#5305 by @tanishqbaweja)
  • Goose on Windows: claude-mem is registered in %APPDATA%\Block\goose\config. (#5323 by @tanishqbaweja)
  • Devin: context is injected again after Devin compacts a session. (#5314 by @patrickballard)
  • Kimi: hook commands stay valid TOML when a path contains a quote. (#5363 by @DeryFerd)
  • Docker: the server and worker share the same BullMQ queue prefix, and the docs list tested Redis-compatible servers. The sync-api container runs as a non-root user. (#5389, #5390 by @murtazayusuf, #5331 by @DeryFerd)
  • Process identity probes are pinned to UTC. (#5312 by @LuckVd)

Import and search

  • Prompt import reads the Codex and OpenCode profiles set by CODEX_HOME and XDG_DATA_HOME, includes committed OpenCode WAL updates, recognizes OpenCode tool inputs as agent-authored, excludes copied Codex fork prompts, decodes JSON tool strings, and keeps hook prompts when session reads are incomplete. (#5303, #5297, #5304, #5300, #5369, #5301 by @rudycelekli)
  • Adoption keeps the real checkout paths before remapping memories, and checkout identity keeps whitespace. (#5296 by @rudycelekli, #5368 by @liuyinchu)
  • Folder searches match relative backslash paths and extensionless files like Makefile. (#5309 by @rudycelekli, #5343 by @liuyinchu)
  • Chroma's semantic fallback query no longer includes doc_type. (#5355 by @0xABCD01)
  • Compact times handle the U+202F separator before AM/PM. (#5356 by @kevin9327)

Privacy and security

  • More secrets are redacted before they are stored: Groq, xAI, NVIDIA NIM, Runcrate, Google OAuth (ya29.), Linear, Notion, Supabase, Sentry, Databricks and HashiCorp Vault tokens. (#5298, #5342, #5345, #5352, #5373 by @kevin9327; #5327 by @DeryFerd)
  • Log values with line breaks are escaped so they can't forge a log record. (#5328 by @DeryFerd)
  • Sync: a 402 verify response drops the cached verdict, the per-isolate auth memo is bounded, and advisory sockets close when their auth verdict lapses. (#5329, #5332 by @DeryFerd; #5358 by @REI-330)
  • Input bounds on batch id arrays, timeline depth, the job listing offset and empty URL modifiers; numeric-string ids are accepted in /api/observations/batch. (#5360, #5362, #5361, #5330 by @DeryFerd; #5341 by @Afloat16; #5336 by @ryrenz)
  • Hermes strips memory tags with a linear scan. (#5320 by @REI-330)

Docs

  • Development prerequisites and test instructions match current tooling; the README lists every built-in language mode; Simplified Chinese and Vietnamese READMEs are synced. (#5324 by @POWDER-RANGER, #5334 by @BaeJinSeop, #5322 by @h6rime, #5391 by @vietmy1711)
  • Test fix for macOS SQLite. (#5313 by @patrickballard)

Thank you to everyone who contributed to this release.

sinelaw/fresh ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v0.5.8fresh-editor 0.5.8 Stable2026-11-06T10:22:11Z

0.5.8

For live updates on Fresh, follow me on X.

Most config options below can be changed in the Settings UI - run Open Settings from the command palette (Ctrl+P).

Features

  • Auto-hide the tab bar - Auto Hide Tab Bar (Settings > Editor > Display) hides it while a single file is open (#3696, requested by @garfunkel)
  • Explorer folder colour can be themed separately from syntax keywords (#3697, requested by @mommysgoodpuppy)
  • Option+Backspace / Option+Delete delete words on macOS (#3643, requested by @gaojunran)
  • Go to Next / Previous Paragraph are in the command palette and Go menu (#2122)
  • Explorer sticky-parent band is shaded so pinned rows read as a distinct strip (#3625, by @PavelLoparev)

Bug Fixes

  • Search/Replace panel is per window - Alt+A in a second window opens its own panel (#3704, reported by @alt-freem)
  • Replace All no longer rewrites a file twice under a symlinked root or tracked symlink (#3720)
  • Find Next ignores matches an edit removed instead of walking stale results (#3464, #3444, reported by @mandolyte)
  • Markdown - Tab honours the configured tab size (#3713, reported by @hbarel) and indents a selection or multiple cursors (#3666, reported by @dnikic)
  • Python match/case and more C++ keywords are highlighted; .c files use the C grammar (#3509, #3644, #3716, reported by @asukaminato0721)
  • Bash highlighting survives f() ( ... ) subshell functions (#3473, #3201, reported by @asukaminato0721)
  • C# typing lag from slow highlighting of interpolated strings is gone (#3667, #3699, reported by @SONickode)
  • LSP servers that declare full text sync receive the whole document, so their diagnostics stay (#3436, reported by @dsvarela)
  • Terminal Shell set from init.ts applies to new terminals (#3712, reported by @brunnerh)
  • Merge conflict resolution no longer leaves an extra blank line per conflict (#3702, #3714, reported by @atk)
  • fresh --diff fixes - stray tab, click past line end, modified markers (#3688, #3689, #3691, #3694, reported by @Korkman)
  • Keys work on macOS when stdin is /dev/tty instead of hanging and spinning a core (#3683, #3693, reported by @StonesFromTheSky)
  • Open File: Enter on a partial dotfile name opens the highlighted directory (#3680, by @StonesFromTheSky)
  • Scrolling Markdown is smoother and idle CPU no longer spins during debounce (#3701)

Internals

  • Reduced flaky tests.

xpzouying/xiaohongshu-mcp ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v2.7.3Release v2.7.3 Stable2026-11-06T07:22:51Z

v2.7.3 修复搜索过程中的风控重定向诊断。搜索页延迟跳到安全验证页或登录页时，会及时返回对应提示，帮助调用方判断需要扫码验证还是重新登录。

  • 在等待搜索结果期间识别延迟重定向，并覆盖结果就绪后、导航后停顿期间的跳转。
  • 按 URL 路径判断页面类型，避免查询参数导致误判。
  • 保留正常空结果行为，补齐本地浏览器回归测试。感谢 @SamYunquChen 的修复与测试贡献。#872
  • 更新贡献者名单和赞赏记录。#909 · #908

提供 macOS Apple Silicon、Windows x64、Linux x64 的 MCP 服务和登录工具，共六个下载文件。

Docker 镜像使用 xpzouying/xiaohongshu-mcp:v2.7.3，可选图形界面镜像使用 xpzouying/xiaohongshu-mcp:v2.7.3-novnc。

构建来自 tag v2.7.3，提交 3b352aa075d63cd5ebae546c9fe7d7d9b81fa7c9，使用 Go 1.27.3、CGO_ENABLED=0 和仓库发布工作流中的构建参数。因目标提交带有 [skip ci]，二进制由本地交叉编译后上传，Docker 镜像由手动触发的发布工作流构建。

View release →

code-yeongyu/oh-my-openagent ↗ 2 releases · 2 stable
v5.1.62 Stable2026-11-06T06:54:57Z

What changed

omo verifies the device service it downloads against the real release key set. The device service's release key set is now pinned to the minted Ed25519 keys, operational first and recovery second, in packages/device-service-core/src/release/release-key.json (a byte copy of the service's own release-key.json). A release signed by either key installs; one signed by neither, or with a malformed signature, is refused before anything is written. Until now the pin was a placeholder that refused every release. (#12437)

Skills and prompts no longer point at skills, files or agents that do not exist, and CI now refuses any new one. The Native ultrawork directive told sessions to read the bun-1-4 skill, which Senpi renamed to bun; it now names bun. remove-deadcode asked for a typescript-programmer skill, which made OpenCode refuse the whole task call; it now loads programming. frontend and ulw-research no longer load an open-design skill that omo never shipped and instead name the nexu-io/open-design library. The ulw-research assembly worker loads imagegen and ulw-loop only when the session lists them, and frontend loads the browser skill where it asked for OpenCode's playwright. OpenCode now ships the hyperplan skill that its hyperplan mode tells the session to load; until now only this repository's own checkout had it. Seven programming references and one frontend cross-reference that pointed at files in the wrong directory, or at a file that never existed, now open the right file. A new check in the root test suite resolves every skill name, skill-relative path, agent and category in shipped skills and prompts for each edition that ships them, and names the closest real target when one is missing. (#12484)

When omo drops the OpenSSL default certificate file (/etc/ssl/cert.pem on macOS) from the engine's trust store, it now tells you why. omo doctor prints an INFO line naming the file, the cause, the certificate block where reading stopped, and whether the drop trusts fewer certificates than plain Bun would. omo trust ensure --json reports the same facts as defaultFileDrop. It is never a warning or a failure. (#12499, part of #10762)

<!-- changes-log: packages/omo-native/changes.md -->

2026-11-05 - Name the OpenSSL default-file drop (#12499, part of #10762)

Design revision 11, 4.4 item 6. readDefaultFileCandidates takes its narrowing flags from the exported DROP_CAUSE_NARROWING table. On a drop, acquireEngineTrust records one openssl-default-file-malformed entry carrying file, cause, blockIndex and narrowing instead of two plain codes, and returns it as defaultFileDrop. runTrustStage prints it in the trust ensure --json line. trust-diagnostics.ts accepts that structured entry and still accepts the plain code for an unreadable file; it refuses another file name, an unknown cause, a non-integer block index or a narrowing flag that does not match the cause. engine-trust-doctor.ts reads the adopted generation's diagnostics.1.jsonl and diagnostics.jsonl, takes the last import-exec or ensure record of that generation, and gives omo doctor one INFO line: the drop, or default-file: kept / unreadable. A missing or torn log yields no line and never fails doctor.

Native skills no longer point Senpi sessions at OpenCode-only agents, tools and commands. A community audit of the installed skills found 16 more references that exist only in OpenCode or nowhere: refactor consulted an oracle agent and called team_list and team_status, debugging ended a team with team_shutdown_request and team_approve_shutdown, frontend loaded skills through a skill tool and addressed a Prometheus planner, ulw-loop named an explorer agent, a plan agent and a /cancel command, ulw-plan offered a git category, and ultimate-browsing named a web-search tool and Playwright MCP calls. Senpi now gets the deep-high lane in place of oracle, the task_send shutdown handshake and task_output in place of the missing team tools, and explore, the ulw-plan workflow and /goal pause or /goal clear in the Native skills. The shared skills read the same in OpenCode and Codex. (#12523)

Changed

  • A Linux container matrix (Debian, Fedora, Alpine, openSUSE Leap and Tumbleweed) now checks the compiled engine's CA import on each distribution: the bundle chosen from os-release is the one the engine reads, a root installed with update-ca-certificates or update-ca-trust is trusted, and a root placed only in openSUSE's /var/lib/ca-certificates/openssl is not. (#12223, part of #10762)

  • The engine CA importer's design 9.7 mutation checks now run in CI for the trust code in packages/omo-native: script/omo-native-trust-mutations.ts applies each mutation as a source patch to a disposable copy of the package, requires its named test to turn red, and requires the same test to pass on the unpatched copy. It covers 31 source mutations (trust policy, launcher trust stage, user extras, generation retention, the OpenSSL hash-directory and default-file selectors) and 2 oracle self-checks, fails when a mutation's patch no longer matches exactly once, and runs in the new Engine trust mutations workflow on every change under packages/omo-native. Rows owned by senpi, the desktop or the macOS and Windows adapters run in their own pipelines. (#12495, part of #10762)

  • Senpi task and LSP tool descriptions now say what the tools do: task_cancel releases a finished child's resident session or queued messages, task_send's 32 KB cap covers team messages only and a message can queue, task_output's tail mode shares the character cap, task_id wins over name, and lsp_find_references, lsp_symbols and lsp_diagnostics state their 200-result cap and defaults. Text only; no behavior change. (#12500, fixes #12496)

Fixed

  • omo trust ensure now reports trust-lease-failed when it cannot refresh the recency of a generation it reuses, instead of silently handing out a generation the retention sweep may remove next. (#12163, part of #10762)

  • On Fedora, RHEL and other systems whose CA bundle carries non-ASCII comment lines between certificates (such as # NetLock Arany (Class Gold) Főtanúsítvány), the compiled engine now imports the system roots instead of discarding the whole bundle as malformed and falling back. Non-ASCII text inside a certificate block still discards the file. (#12220, part of #10762)

  • A device whose memory-sync sign-in ends (revoked, signed out, omo logout, omo account forget --device, or replaced by a new sign-in) no longer keeps its sync trust pin, so omo account show --account stops reporting it as joined and re-pairing after a reset is no longer blocked. Joining again always goes through the pairing or recovery ceremony. The new omo account retire-sync --account <id> does the same for a desktop-owned session. (#12280)

  • omo doctor now reports a generated omo wrapper whose cached runtime is gone instead of passing over it in silence: WARN broken omo on PATH: <file> launches <missing path>; remove it, with no repair installer offered for the broken wrapper. It also warns when two different omo-ai installs (for example bun and npm) are both on PATH, where previously only installs of different kinds were flagged. Broken detection does not run for Windows .cmd wrappers: they write rem OMO_GENERATED_RUNTIME_WRAPPER while the check looks for the # marker. (#12534, fixes #12110)

<!-- changes-log: packages/omo-native/changes.md -->

2026-11-05 - Doctor reports a broken omo wrapper and duplicate omo-ai installs (#12534)

omo doctor now reports a generated omo wrapper whose cached runtime is gone, and warns when two different omo-ai installs are both on PATH. Windows .cmd wrappers are not covered by the broken check: they write rem OMO_GENERATED_RUNTIME_WRAPPER while the check looks for the # marker.

Known issues

  • Engine TLS trust v1: distrusted intermediate certificates under an allowed root are not yet blocked; tracked upstream. The compiled engine now has tests for every TLS path (global fetch, Bun.fetch, a saved fetch, a Worker fetch, node:https and WebSocket): a root outside the selected set or denied by the OS fails on all of them, a store intermediate is used only when its usage allows server authentication and it is not denied, and a test named limitation-pin: records that a denied intermediate the server itself presents under an allowed root is still accepted. (#12219, part of #10762)

v5.1.61 Stable2026-11-05T19:01:35Z

Runs on senpi 2026.11.3-3: Claude Opus and Fable get their full system prompt back (the pre-diet wording; every other model keeps the slimmer prompt), Anthropic Subscription no longer refuses a working Claude login as blocked when an older omo process is still running or when Claude Code can't read its login behind a locked keychain, a usage limit with an ISO reset time blocks that account until the reset instead of retrying every minute, a goal waits for the first limited account to come back instead of spending turns, and Shift+Enter inserts a newline in macOS Terminal.app. (#12311)

Runs on senpi 2026.11.3-3: the engine socket can now send compact message updates (compact_message_updates_v1) with event_resync recovery, so long streamed turns cost less to relay. It stays off unless SENPI_ENGINE_SOCKET_COMPACT_UPDATES=1 is set. (Part of code-yeongyu/senpi#4272) (#12215)

macOS computer use keeps the display awake while it operates, and says when the screen is locked or asleep. The engine now holds a PreventUserIdleDisplaySleep power assertion ("OmO computer use is operating the desktop" in pmset -g assertions) from the first request other than capabilities, control.state and stopPath.* until 60 seconds after the last one; session.close, a disconnect and a stop release it at once. capabilities.displayAsleep reports when every display of the session sleeps, omo doctor prints it on the display line, and a CaptureFailed, InputFailed or Timeout message ends with (the screen is locked) or (the display is asleep) when that is the likely cause. Other platforms are unchanged. (#11530, part of #11466)

Fixed

  • In the desktop's first-run setup, "Understanding you" no longer stops with "No retained scan with this id" after About you brings anything in. Applying an onboarding scan now keeps it available to understanding until the scan expires; a scan is still applied only once. (#12509, fixes #12508)
  • The engine now registers its agent dir with the device service even when the service's state directory appears right after the engine started (for example, when the device service is installed or started at the same moment). A freshly armed directory watch can miss an entry created in its first milliseconds, so the registrant never noticed the service and other devices listed no sessions. Each watch arm now re-checks the path once after it settles. (#12482)
  • Your other signed-in devices now see this device's sessions after the OmO device service starts or restarts. Every omo engine, in the terminal and the ones the desktop app starts, keeps its own agent directory registered with the device service and registers it again when the service comes back or the device is signed in again; it never holds an engine open and stays off with "device_service": { "enabled": false }. (#12380, fixes #12379)
  • A reflection sandbox probe that timed out once (for example on a busy machine) no longer turns the sandbox off until restart. Reflection checks again on its next run, so it runs sandboxed again once bwrap responds. A machine where bwrap definitively cannot create a sandbox is still checked only once (#12376). A reflection run that falls back to running unsandboxed because bwrap is unusable at that moment is now logged once as a degraded sandbox, and once more when the sandbox comes back, instead of passing silently. (#12378)
  • A session whose Stop was released but whose background children stayed paused, refusing every new task with "Task admission is held by your Stop", is released by the next message you send. /resume and new prompts used to do nothing because the session no longer held a Stop, so only /reload or a restart cleared it (#12342). (#12343)
  • Revoking a memory-sync device, omo logout, or signing out on the desktop no longer freezes every other device's pushes. A remaining device now rotates the account memory key so the removed device is excluded, and it can no longer read notes written after it was removed. While a key change is pending, the status shows sync as blocked instead of claiming it is synced. (#12280) (#12302)
  • The dev-browser CDP-port test "with no grace window it still runs cleanly under set -u" now waits for the port holder to exit before it checks that the holder received SIGTERM. Under host load it could fail even though the release worked (#12287). (#12293)
  • Memory sync replicates between devices again. The client refused every batch row from GET /v1/sync/entries that carried the batch_id the service contract declares, so two devices paired but no edit reached the other. Account recovery, and pairing a device after the first batch, failed the same way. A well-formed batch_id on a batch row is now accepted. A null or malformed one, one on a keyring row, and any other extra field are still refused (#12277). (#12292)
  • Background task children that a Stop paused are resumed again when you type after the Stop. The run's Resume used to miss them because the Stop's own run fences raised the session's pause sequence, so they stayed paused for hours while the task widget showed them as running or starting (#12249). (#12268)
  • A paused child that its run's Resume cannot restore is retried a few times and then ends as an error the parent is told about, instead of staying paused forever. Its transcript is kept for task_output. (#12268)
  • The task widget shows a paused child as paused, with no running clock, and /task-kill offers paused children. (#12268)
  • Memory reflection children started inside the Linux bubblewrap sandbox no longer exit at startup with unsafe trust ancestor. Inside the sandbox's user namespace, root-owned directories show the overflow uid. The engine trust stage now accepts one only when the launcher verified, from outside the sandbox, that this exact inode is root-owned. A directory owned by another user is still refused. The sandbox child also gets its own private TMPDIR, and a read-only trust directory falls back to a private temporary one. A hand-made user namespace that omo cannot verify now gets the named refusal trust-userns-unattested with a next step, instead of a stack trace. (#12079, code-yeongyu/senpi#5334)
  • RPC liveness tests keep pre-existing async work on the real clock, so unrelated timers cannot be mistaken for leaked session timers during confirmed close. The regression injects a foreign timer while the session is terminal-pending and retains every close and cleanup assertion. (#11937, fixes #11934)
  • Keep buffered logging on its own scheduler and cancellation pair so virtual clocks used by RPC callers cannot capture log flushes (#11687, landing the work from #11641)
  • A disposed process child no longer sends anything or arms a timer after its teardown: a late abort, steer, finished-turn check or state probe is refused at once instead of waiting out a 10-30 s request deadline, an extension UI request it sends after teardown gets no automatic answer written back, a closed-pipe error reported after teardown no longer arms the exit grace timer, and a heartbeat probe whose launch lands after its heartbeat stopped is skipped (#11687, part of #11673)
  • A memory session's shutdown now waits for every memory read still running in its identity directory: the compiled-block and recall-corpus caches, every memory hook and memory tool call in flight (a prompt turn, a settle, a memory tool write), the bind-time reconcile, and detached dream requests are all tracked, so no git child outlives session_shutdown and Windows no longer fails the next removal of that directory with EBUSY (#11671, part of #11135; the probe case is #11136)

Changed

  • Thread tools no longer refuse a chain of messages at its fifth hop, at 64 messages, or when it comes back to a session it passed (A to B to A): a chain has no hidden count limit. A chain continued after its 7-day root lifetime is delivered under a fresh root instead of being refused. A session still cannot message itself. (#12331, part of #12320)
  • Thread tools and subagent, workflow, workpool and team tools declare that they run on the loop side of a split session (runs: "brain"); sessions that are not split behave exactly as before. (#12324, part of #12320)
  • The OmO Native extension's main bundle is about 27.9 KB smaller: the two ultrawork directive variants store their shared text once instead of twice. Both directives are unchanged. (#12291)

Added

  • Every interactive omo start now sets up this device's OmO device service in the background once you are signed in, so your other signed-in devices can see and command its sessions. It never delays the start and speaks up only when it stops on something you can fix. Turn it off with "device_service": { "enabled": false } in omo.json, or keep it and stop other devices from controlling this one with omo remote control off (omo remote control shows the setting). (#12257, part of #12051)

View release →

stablyai/orca ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v1.4.251 Stable2026-11-06T06:31:44Z

Richer phone chats, add-to-chat from anywhere & steadier terminals ✨

  • 📱 Much more chat on the phone: Rewind to an earlier message, continue a stopped reply, pin the agent's plan above the composer, paste images and long text, manage a chat's goal and see context usage, with unsent drafts kept across an app restart.
  • 📎 Add anything to chat: Send a selection from a file, a Markdown preview or a terminal to any agent in the workspace, or start a new agent with it.
  • 🔁 Chats pick up after a restart: New installs resume working chats automatically after Orca quits or updates, and resuming is more reliable for everyone who has it on.
  • 🤖 Workers can start their own workers: Nested workers are allowed by default, finished workers can keep messaging their coordinator, and stopping a worker stops its helpers.
  • ⚡ Faster everyday work: Launch skips a second login-shell capture, source control and the tab bar do less repeated work, and long chats stay responsive.
  • 🖥️ Steadier terminals: Terminals re-attach after a daemon reconnect, survive a busy app, and new GPU canvases no longer flash blank.
  • 🗂️ Easier project organization: Collapse or expand all projects, create a child worktree from the context menu and refresh folder project groups.

Highlights

  • Chat: Add a file, Markdown preview or terminal selection to a chat. Agent messages use one-line cards on desktop and phone, native chats appear in Cmd+J recents and title search, and Cmd/Ctrl+Enter on an empty composer steers the first queued message. Claude chats keep effort changeable after the first reply and clear a stuck "needs permission" status when Claude ends the request itself. Codex chats show /usage and subagent task names.
  • Phone: Rewind and continue chats, pin the plan, paste images and long text, set and manage goals, see the context usage ring, follow background tasks, copy or share images, copy file paths, choose where links open, and sign in from a signed-out chat.
  • Orchestration: Workers can start their own workers, keep messaging their coordinator after they finish, and stop their helpers when stopped. Mail waits for a draft you are typing, including in OpenCode and after a restart.
  • Performance: Startup, source control conflict badges and branch compares, the tab bar, browser login detection, Pi terminal spawns and plugin catalog images all do less repeated work. Long chats no longer rescan their whole history on every status update. Chats with ACP agents also stop holding on to finished tool and message records for the chat's whole life.
  • Terminals & remote hosts: Terminals re-attach after a daemon reconnect and keep their link through a main-process freeze. SSH explains when a host's server setup stops on a known cause and stops restarting a server that keeps crashing. Server actions reconnect a disconnected server.

Compatibility & upgrade notes

  • Nested worker depth: The default is now 4. Existing profiles set to 1 move to 4 on upgrade; you can set it back in Settings, and Orca keeps that choice.
  • Resume working chats after restart: On by default for new installs only. Existing users keep their saved choice, and existing profiles that never chose stay off.
  • Claude permission status: Clearing a "needs permission" status that Claude ended itself needs Claude 2.1.118 or later. Orca installs one more managed Claude hook for it; older Claude versions keep the previous behavior.
  • macOS updates: Macs below a release's minimum macOS stay on their last compatible build and get a one-time notice. The minimum is still macOS 12, so nothing changes yet.
  • Chat UI menu: "Fork Agent Session…" is no longer in Chat UI's menu. Terminals keep it.

neovim/neovim ↗ 1 release · 1 prerelease
nightlyNvim development (prerelease) build Prerelease2026-11-06T05:18:14Z 
NVIM v0.13.0-dev-2032+g254bec339c
Build type: RelWithDebInfo
LuaJIT 2.1.1793383622

Release notes

  • Changelog (fixes + features)
  • News (:help news in Nvim)

Install

Windows

Zip

  1. Download nvim-win64.zip (or nvim-win-arm64.zip for ARM)
  2. Extract the zip
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

MSI

  1. Download nvim-win64.msi (or nvim-win-arm64.msi for ARM)
  2. Run the MSI
  3. Run nvim.exe in your terminal

Note: On Windows "Server" you may need to install vcruntime*.dll.

macOS (x86_64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-x86_64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-x86_64/bin/nvim

macOS (arm64)

  1. Download nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Run xattr -c ./nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz (to avoid "unknown developer" warning)
  3. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-macos-arm64.tar.gz
  4. Run ./nvim-macos-arm64/bin/nvim

Linux (x86_64)

If your system does not have the required glibc version, try the (unsupported) builds for older glibc.

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-x86_64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-x86_64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-x86_64/bin/nvim

Linux (arm64)

AppImage

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
  2. Run chmod u+x nvim-linux-arm64.appimage && ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage
    • If your system does not have FUSE you can extract the appimage:
      ./nvim-linux-arm64.appimage --appimage-extract
./squashfs-root/usr/bin/nvim

Tarball

  1. Download nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  2. Extract: tar xzvf nvim-linux-arm64.tar.gz
  3. Run ./nvim-linux-arm64/bin/nvim

Other

  • Install by package manager

View release →

can1357/oh-my-pi ↗ 1 release · 1 stable
v18.17.0 Stable2026-11-06T05:12:54Z

@oh-my-pi/pi-agent-core

Added

  • Added Agent.queuedMessageGate to filter queued messages before delivery and observe claimed messages returned undelivered.
  • Added wireToolsFingerprint(), returning a hash of the tool specs sent with a request.

@oh-my-pi/pi-ai

Added

  • Added opt-in standard Responses WebSockets for catalog-supported models, with incremental turns and automatic HTTP/SSE fallback; enable through OMP settings (#17354 by @TatsukiMeng)
  • AuthStorage can now be constructed over an AuthStorageBackend instead of a credential store, so another thread's instance can serve every call
  • Anthropic official-API sessions use message threads, sending only new messages per turn for faster first tokens; toggle with statefulResponses: false or PI_ANTHROPIC_MESSAGE_THREADS=0/1
  • Added devin-cli (Devin CLI seat key) and devin-windsurf (Windsurf Enterprise sign-in) logins, both stored as a devin API key (#13637 by @pavel-kalmykov, #13288 by @pavel-kalmykov)
  • Factory Droid Gemini 4 Argon requests now match Droid's request and conversation handling (#17861 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • Changing the Codex ultrafast service tier now opens a connection for the selected tier, so requests route correctly
  • Factory Droid requests and login now match the current Droid client
  • Codex HTTP requests (turns, compaction, image generation, web search, model discovery) no longer send OpenAI-Beta: responses=experimental, matching the official Codex client

Fixed

  • Fixed account switching stopping at rate-limited accounts; other eligible stored accounts are now tried first
  • Fixed OpenRouter Decisions judges ignoring compat.openRouterRouting and compat.extraBody, including @provider pins (#18140)
  • OAuth broker now validates residency, inference-region, and selected-organization metadata in uploaded credentials and remote snapshots
  • Fixed Muse Code subscription sessions failing with 403 client_attestation_denied when tools were available (#17435, #17515 by @harshav167, continued in #18053 by @abilliontokens)
  • Fixed xai-tts failing with 422 missing field language; language now defaults to auto (#17992 by @Mirceone)
  • Fixed Codex WebSocket turns sending malformed incremental input after reconnects or onPayload edits, and prewarming busy sockets (#17809 by @xiangnan0811)
  • API-key logins now store keys under the auth rule's store-as provider (#13637 by @pavel-kalmykov)
  • Fixed openai-responses, Codex, and Muse Code continuations after an output limit cut a tool call, including reasoning-first models (#17847, #18189 by @mttzzz)
  • Gemini and Vertex malformed-response finishes now report the correct stop reason and keep token usage; RPC prompt errors are reported as retryable
  • Fixed reranking when Cohere-compatible responses omit the model field
  • Reranking requests now send configured model headers, including custom Authorization
  • Provider retries no longer retry responses explicitly marked non-retryable

@oh-my-pi/pi-catalog

Added

  • Added tool-name-prefix compat axis for Responses hosts that reject certain tool names; Muse Code sets it to _ (#17435, #17515 by @harshav167, continued in #18053 by @abilliontokens)
  • Added supports-message-threads compat axis (supportsMessageThreads), enabled for Claude 4.5+ on first-party Anthropic
  • Added opt-in Responses WebSocket capability via OMP settings or SDK preferences, off by default (#17354 by @TatsukiMeng)
  • Added Factory Droid Gemini 4 Argon on Global inference for accounts with the feature flag (#17861 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added Factory Droid Muse Spark 1.3 (Global region, muse_spark_1_3 flag) with low to xhigh effort (#17862 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

  • Updated Factory Droid client version reporting; improved tool-input streaming for Claude via Azure Foundry

Fixed

  • Fixed newly published Azure models missing after catalog refresh (#18173, #18174 by @dunkpi)
  • Fixed Factory Droid hiding models when the live-routing family default yields no usable upstream; explicit empty per-model routes still hide them (#17862 by @will-bogusz)

@oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent

Added

  • Bash commands, foreground and background, now show a live execution visualization in Raw or Python-like Pseudo view, with variable values and replay history; disable with display.shellVisualization (default on).
  • grep and glob inside an ovnfs checkout ask the ovnfs daemon to search the checkout in-process, avoiding per-file mount round trips; the daemon and its mount come from ovnfs --json status when the ovnfs binary is found. They fall back to the native search when the daemon is unavailable or rejects the request, and cancelling the tool cancels the daemon's search. ovnfs with admin protocol 5 is required.
  • Added skills.manualSkills glob patterns to hide skills from the model catalog while keeping them manually invocable (#18114 by @Shadorain)
  • Added Keenable web search (web/keenable), using KEENABLE_API_KEY or a stored keenable credential, or a keyless public endpoint when explicitly selected (#17953 by @KGKallasmaa)
  • web_search results list failed providers with reason and status code in details.failures (#18124 by @will-bogusz)
  • Added opt-in background auto-update (update.auto, offered in setup) that installs new releases via your install method and runs them next launch; skipped on Nix, source checkouts, and Tern.
  • omp update refuses to run while another update (including auto-update) is installing.
  • Added none to edit.mode (and PI_EDIT_VARIANT) to remove the edit tool; bash stays available even if bash.enabled is off.
  • Added default-off providers.openaiResponsesWebsockets to use WebSockets for supported standard Responses models (#17354 by @TatsukiMeng)
  • Pinned sessions show a pin beside their title; /unpin [session id] unpins a session (#17848 by @SuperUserA)
  • Extensions can draw tool calls and message/event blocks full, compact, or hidden via pi.registerTranscriptPresenter(), and redraw with ctx.ui.refreshTranscript(ids); enable with extensionRenderers.transcriptPresenters (default off) (#17960 by @materemias) (#17997 by @materemias)
  • Consecutive compact transcript blocks render as tight rows without blank gaps; extension renderers opt in via isTranscriptBlockCompact() (#17997 by @materemias)
  • Added a fullscreen link browser for links in scrollback: press Alt+O again (or Page Up) while link tags are shown (#16476 by @materemias)
  • Added a cache status-line segment (full/nerd presets) showing whether the next message hits the prompt cache; Tern and /session show why not and how much is resent (#12677) (#17496 by @will-bogusz)
  • ^ model tags accept a :level suffix (e.g. ^openai-codex/gpt-6-astra:high) to set the subagent's effort (overriding task.maxEffort), with completion of each model's supported levels (#17574 by @a-glapinski)
  • Added the chillax magic keyword for brief, lowest-effort thinking on supported models; configure via magicKeywords.chillax
  • Added tui.colorfulMarkdown (on by default): replies color headings by level, bold and italic text, inline code and table headers from the theme's palette; turn it off for the previous single heading color and plain bold/italic
  • Added tui.motion (full/reduced/none), tui.spinnerInterval (0 = static) and tui.maxFps to control animation and repaint rate, also via --motion/--spinner-interval/--max-fps and PI_MOTION/PI_SPINNER_INTERVAL/PI_MAX_FPS; an explicit tui.spinnerInterval beats tui.motion, and keystroke echo keeps 30 fps (#14240 by @mccraigmccraig)
  • Added omp zsh, omp bash and omp sh to run omp's built-in shell engine directly, passing all arguments through and exiting with the shell's status.
  • omp zsh/omp bash keep shell history in your $HISTFILE, compatible with real zsh, including ! expansion, fc and history.
  • omp zsh runs interactively like zsh (line editor, bindkey, completion, job control, prompt hooks, common modules), so oh-my-zsh and powerlevel10k configs start.
  • RPC bash results report truncated output via elidedBytes/elidedLines and columnTruncated*/columnMax fields, decoded by the Go, Python and Rust SDKs (#17957 by @H4vC)
  • Added pi.getShortcutConflict(chord) so extensions can check whether a key chord is free or who holds it (#17961 by @materemias)
  • Exported unwrapHashlineHeaderPath from @oh-my-pi/pi-coding-agent to turn a [path#TAG] target into its plain path (#17961 by @materemias)
  • devin-cli login imports Devin CLI credentials, so the devin provider no longer requires DEVIN_API_KEY (#13637 by @pavel-kalmykov)
  • devin-windsurf login authenticates the devin provider with an existing Windsurf Enterprise account (#13288 by @pavel-kalmykov)

Changed

  • ovnfs turns on by itself when its binary is found (ovnfs.path, else ovnfs on PATH): agents get the separate-checkout guidance, and isolated subagents use ovnfs checkouts when isolation.backend is auto
  • Text Verbosity now defaults to low and applies to Codex too, matching the official Codex client: shorter answers that finish sooner; set textVerbosity to medium for the old length
  • A cost cap set on the main thread now applies to running subagents immediately
  • RPC wire protocol and task streaming have lower serialization overhead
  • Local Whisper speech-to-text, Parakeet/Nemotron speech, and the local tiny model install their runtimes on first use, shrinking installs; first use needs network access
  • wait with nothing new returns after 5s (backing off to 5m over repeated idle waits) instead of blocking up to 30 minutes, and nudges the agent toward other work
  • wait returns jobs finishing within 1.5s of each other in one result
  • Broadcasts and progress notes no longer cut short wait or interrupt bash/eval; a direct message still ends a wait and delivers every queued message
  • wait for a reply gives up after 5 minutes (was 30) and returns at once if the other agent is waiting on the user
  • In Tern, streamed assistant text and thinking are sent unpaced, cutting omp's CPU use; other terminals keep the smooth reveal
  • Source-checkout sessions start faster; existing build stamps remain readable
  • The agent's bash tool never touches your shell history or completion, and its zsh -i/bash -i skip .zshrc/.bashrc
  • Embedded Bash follows Bash 5.3 semantics and preserves supported aliases
  • CLI flags, session-only settings writes, and SDK overrides now outrank a setting's env var (PI_MOTION=none omp --motion full uses full motion); env vars still outrank settings files
  • JS and Python tool aborts now report the original abort reason
  • Many concurrent subagents now run in worker threads so they don't slow the TUI; new task.workerThreads caps workers (default 4; 0 keeps all on the main thread)
  • Interactive startup reaches the first frame ~75ms sooner when skills are listed in the system prompt
  • "What's New" release notes now appear just after the first frame; quitting before it keeps them for the next start
  • Subagents can no longer broadcast (write agent://all fails, pointing to a direct agent://<id> message) and are no longer told to announce shared-file edits; the main agent can still broadcast
  • Factory Droid Gemini 4 Argon sessions keep earlier turns intact across save and reload, with correct image clamping (#17861 by @will-bogusz)

Fixed

  • On macOS, the browser tool's headless Chromium keeps background tabs at normal CPU priority, so on a busy machine tab.traceStart(), opening a second tab, and other calls into background tabs no longer stall or time out
  • Subagents loading MCP tools (such as Kata's load_*) no longer change the main session's tools or system prompt
  • Mounting an MCP tool mid-session on prefix-bound models no longer changes the eval tool description, and the mount notice now reaches the model
  • Worker-thread subagents are faster: switching views on long transcripts no longer lags, wait no longer streams full transcripts, and they stall less on a busy main thread
  • Streaming a large batch task call no longer slows quadratically (about 80 ms → 2 ms for a 200 KB call)
  • computer from Eval now uses zoom for legible close-ups instead of cropping the downscaled screenshot (#18158 by @will-bogusz)
  • Python eval @tool definitions with type annotations now register on Python 3.9 (#18143 by @Winston-Hsiao)
  • read of an SEC EDGAR filing document or folder returns that document, not the company profile (#18150 by @will-bogusz)
  • read of a Read the Docs page no longer returns another repository's linked file (#18162 by @will-bogusz)
  • A browser click beside a direct tab.waitForDownload() now errors with a tab.run suggestion that actually catches the download (#17615) (#18155 by @will-bogusz)
  • Browser open({ viewport }) now sizes relay tabs, app.cdp_url/app.path browsers, and headed Chromium on first open (#18160 by @will-bogusz)
  • Discarded empty, reasoning-only, or length-stop turns still count billed usage in session totals and omp stats (#18178)
  • /extensions shows the active ~/.agents skill when a higher-priority ~/.claude copy is not opted in (#18190)
  • Tool results whose details contain a throwing getter no longer silently stop all later session saves (#18134 by @MonsieurBarti)
  • write to a new ssh:// file no longer fails when the content ends with a read truncation notice (#18154 by @will-bogusz)
  • find returns verified hits as partial results when its 20 s budget runs out instead of failing (#14809) (#18161 by @will-bogusz)
  • Background bash completion notices name the command, so parallel jobs are distinguishable (#12607) (#18166 by @will-bogusz)
  • LSP results stay current for open files edited outside OMP, and files unused for 5 minutes are closed in the language server (#18153 by @will-bogusz)
  • Markdown edits no longer falsely warn that valid text (e.g. | a |||) fails to parse (#18151 by @will-bogusz)
  • Bash arguments like "skill:// external command" reach external commands as written instead of failing with exit 126 (#18163 by @will-bogusz)
  • Thin tool images are padded to the vision model's minimum size instead of causing a 400 (#18081)
  • JSON query truncation notices report the 50KB read budget instead of the bytes shown (#18022 by @eggpeat)
  • Extension links, renderers, and ctx.ui.getLinkClicksAvailable() now work on every launch, not only the first after an edit (#18116 by @materemias)
  • Multi-line AST Grep captures show on one meta: row instead of splitting across rows (#18105 by @JustasMasiulis)
  • Extension shortcuts now match regardless of modifier order or spelling: reserved chords stay ignored and the last-loaded duplicate wins (#17961 by @materemias)
  • Building from source works again (#18074 by @Maziar123)
  • ./c continue no longer reports "Nothing to resume" after /handoff, compaction, or a /skill:-started session
  • omp bash and omp sh show a prompt when PS1 is unset
  • display.cacheMissMarker now flags turns that re-send most of the conversation (e.g. after an idle gap), even when the system prompt block was cached
  • A running session keeps its setting values when config.yml gains a value its omp version doesn't know, preserving tools and prompt cache
  • Forcing a queued message with Enter now reliably resumes the agent
  • Interrupting a Python eval cell blocked on top-level await cancels the cell and keeps the kernel and variables (#18115 by @Winston-Hsiao)
  • Apostrophes in slash-command and prompt-template arguments (e.g. /fix the user's login bug) now reach $ARGUMENTS and $1, $2, … intact (#18008)
  • Bash output: middle truncation no longer splits lines and reports accurate line ranges; silent success returns (no output, exit code 0) (#17957 by @H4vC)
  • A new session's first frame always shows your configured default model and thinking level, not one from another session or folder
  • System-prompt skill listings show the compressed routing hint instead of a truncated description when it fits 160 characters; previously skipped skills are recompressed
  • Skill descriptions stop triggering background model requests once the session-tree cost cap is reached, and their spend counts toward the cap (#18016)
  • The idle recap survives resume, restart, and transcript rebuilds, staying at the end until the conversation moves on (#18113 by @materemias)
  • Chat sessions on a web (or other model-kind) role model no longer fall back to web/* search providers and fail with Unhandled API in mapOptionsForApi: web-search (#18056 by @haesol-shin)
  • Grouped read rows naming a URL link the whole URL, not just its last path segment (#18071 by @iacore)
  • A second ask note on a later single-select question no longer replaces the previous question's note (#17979)
  • The first run of a compiled binary after install or upgrade no longer fails omp --smoke-test with SubagentCodeChangedError or keeps subagents on the main thread (#18060)
  • Retrying an aborted turn keeps the unsent composer draft (#17977 by @Shadorain)
  • The tts tool uses Grok Voice again instead of silently falling back to Kokoro, and sends language to xAI (auto-detected if omitted) (#17992 by @Mirceone)
  • Windows bash output on non-UTF-8 code pages (CJK) no longer turns into mojibake after one invalid byte; the byte shows as � (#17947 by @H4vC)
  • Hindsight no longer silently drops the last mental model (often User Preferences) from the prompt when over the render budget (#17548, #17955 by @harshaygadekar)
  • Subagents woken by IRC, restored, revived, or rerun by a workpool keep their task.softRequestBudget guard; it and task.agentSoftRequestBudget resolve by agent name (e.g. scout), not task ID (#17988 by @sinhduc01, #18121 by @eggpeat)
  • A focused subagent's blocked indicator follows its own approval mode, tool policies, and grants instead of the main session's
  • Bash tool: exec >file, exec 2>/dev/null, or exec <file no longer redirects later commands in the session
  • Bash tool: an EXIT trap fires once at the end of its command, not after every later command
  • Bash tool: late output from a background job ((sleep 1; echo late) &) no longer appears in a later command's output
  • Bash tool: env -i no longer passes non-shell-name variables (A-B=1), and X=v env -i/X=v env -u X no longer pass an exported outer X
  • Bash tool: xargs reports a signal-killed command like GNU xargs: xargs: CMD: terminated by signal N, stopping with status 125
  • macOS: starting a bash tool session no longer briefly sets umask to 022, which could give new files wrong permissions
  • Bash tool: a shebang-less executable script run in the background from a FIFO (./script < fifo &) runs as bash instead of failing with Exec format error
  • Subagents no longer hang in an async Python @tool call waiting on an asyncio.Lock held by another subagent's call
  • Cancelling a subagent mid-call cancels its async Python @tool coroutine, releasing locks, and reports Python tool request was cancelled
  • Agent Hub no longer shows a previous session's subagents after creating or switching sessions
  • ast_grep and ast_edit detect languages for uppercase file extensions
  • snapcompact no longer treats incompatible older archives as reductions
  • computer window lookup and app opening work for app IDs, paths, and ~ paths, and survive Wayland permission timeouts
  • Embedded headless Chrome no longer outlives its host process, and computer/browser lifecycle works in SDK embeddings and local test runs
  • Out-of-credit provider errors now fail immediately instead of retrying for long periods; Retry works promptly once the account is funded.
  • DeepSeek-style *** End and *** End Patch markers are no longer written into files or truncate later edits
  • Composer prediction ghost text no longer vanishes when advisor messages arrive

@oh-my-pi/pi-mnemopi

Changed

  • Development installs reuse fastembed’s ONNX Runtime instead of installing a duplicate native runtime.

@oh-my-pi/pi-natives

Breaking Changes

  • Replaced parseShell() with RunTrace.present() and ShellVisualization.

Added

  • Added RunTrace: command states, variable assignments, per-producer output, bounded replay, Raw/Pseudo presentation, and restoring persisted traces.
  • Added obash --trace=PATH to write a JSON execution trace at exit; UnitCompiled events now include source node tables.
  • Shell and executeShell now run pi-shell's Bash engine with Bash 5.3 semantics, including aliases containing pipelines, subshells, and separators.
  • Native shell is faster across parsing, functions, expansion, redirections, arithmetic, loops, and external command startup.
  • Sixel image output now uses a built-in encoder.
  • Built-in ps -o accepts Apple's utime (alias putime) for user CPU time, instead of failing with unknown output format specifier 'utime'.
  • Shell spawn benchmarks now report median and p99 latency.

Changed

  • Built-in jq now reports errors as plain text with line:column locations.
  • Work-profile flamegraphs are now static SVGs with hoverable titles.
  • Built-in grep -r, rg, find and fd are faster on small and large trees and stream output to pipes in batches.

Fixed

  • Fixed a crash on Linux when a zsh-mode sort or strftime/$langinfo named a locale that is not installed.
  • Fixed env -i in the bash tool passing exported shell functions (BASH_FUNC_name%%) and non-environment shell variables to the command.
  • Fixed $! in command substitutions, subshells and pipeline stages to name the parent's last background job, as in Bash (#18107 by @sjawhar)
  • Fixed built-in local rsync output on a busy machine sometimes differing from rsync 3.5.1: --delete/info lines from subdirectories could print after the top-level names and the byte totals in sent … received …/--stats could be off by 4.
  • Fixed Windows rsync treating a one-letter daemon host like h::module as a drive path (#18048 by @jchanghong023)
  • Fixed git, its helpers and commit hooks started by omp reading the terminal, which could swallow keystrokes and leave Tern protocol text like _tsp;e;{"ev":"ack",…} in the composer; they no longer inherit the terminal or share its session.
  • Fixed ssh started by the built-in rsync (and sort --compress-program) prompting on the user's terminal, e.g. host-key confirmations appearing after exit; they no longer share omp's terminal or session.
  • Fixed history saves hanging when a stale lock cannot be removed.
  • Fixed native addon startup on glibc 2.17–2.28, which lacks posix_spawn_file_actions_addchdir_np (#18046 by @jchanghong023)
  • Fixed Windows rmdir accepting trailing-dot or trailing-space operands that could remove a different directory (#18047 by @jchanghong023)
  • Fixed timeout, nohup, git and similar utilities inside shell functions skipping operands or running the function's arguments (#18030 by @sjawhar)
  • Fixed native Windows commands not resolving executables via PATH/PATHEXT, and absolute paths being prefixed with the cwd (#18041 by @jchanghong023)
  • Fixed minimized git stash pop and git status counting status lines and "Dropped" notices as untracked files (#18159 by @eggpeat)
  • Fixed associative arrays slowing quadratically: 100,000 inserts and lookups drop from ~90 s to ~0.1 s.
  • Fixed GTK3 Open/Save dialogs on Linux: ax() and element input work, and Wayland window lists include them (#18011 by @will-bogusz)
  • capabilities() now reports input: false and inputPermission: "unavailable" on Wayland without a RemoteDesktop portal (e.g. sway); input fails immediately (#18012 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed .bzl files being treated as Python instead of Starlark in ast-grep, astGrep, astEdit, and code summaries (#18145 by @eggpeat)
  • Minimized rustfmt --check output now reports how many files need formatting instead of miscounting diff locations (#18147 by @will-bogusz)
  • Minimized git diff, git show and diff -r now list binary files (as Bin), pure renames and mode-only changes (#18148 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Wayland el.type and el.press(keys) shifting the app's focus before refusing; they now refuse first (#17915 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Windows background drags in Tk apps reporting success while doing nothing; they now follow computer.backgroundFallback (#17913 by @will-bogusz)
  • Built-in rsync now matches rsync 3.5.1 for unopenable --log-file errors; empty --log-file= logs to syslog (#17980 by @sjawhar)
  • Fixed X11 takeover and raise() failing on a window previously targeted by background typing, e.g. under Openbox (#17910 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed X11 background keys into Qt 5/6 apps silently doing nothing; they now use takeover, or refuse when computer.backgroundFallback is refuse (#17911 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed Wayland doubleClick and click with count registering as a single click in KDE Plasma Qt apps (#17903 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed press(), perform() and menu.select() failing on Linux in browsers, VS Code, LibreOffice and non-English locales; press() now toggles toggle/radio buttons (#17936 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed computer.focusedWindow(), ax() and observe() on GNOME/KDE Wayland reading a shell surface instead of the focused app (#17922 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed takeover keys into iPhone Mirroring and Screen Sharing leaving Fn held, dropping Shift and later letters (#17909 by @will-bogusz)
  • An unanswered Wayland RemoteDesktop permission dialog now fails input with Timeout before the run deadline, sending nothing (#17932 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed X11 takeover input stopping with Cancelled after you typed locally; Escape now stops computer use for the rest of the turn (#17912 by @will-bogusz)
  • Fixed X11 background el.type/el.press raising the window and taking your keyboard; elements without GTK 3 focus now use takeover (#17921 by @will-bogusz)

Changed

Fixed
